# Yellowpages

Categorized index of 1347 repositories starred by [@AIimagined](https://github.com/AIimagined?tab=stars). Repos with the same purpose sit in the same group, sorted by stars. Nothing is cloned here: every entry links to the original repository.

> Generated by `build.py`. Do not edit by hand: change `categories.json` or `overrides.json` instead.

**Last updated:** 🟢 under 3 months ago · 🟡 under 1 year · 🔴 over 1 year · 📦 archived  
**License:** MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD = free for commercial use · GPL, AGPL = changes must be open-sourced · None, Custom = check with the author before using  
**🔗** = project website or demo
